([Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot](https://github.com/Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus) · ★1)
- **gitnexus**: Use GitNexus repository graphs to trace symbols, call paths, and change impact before editing unfamiliar or cross-cutting code. ([Thirumalai-Tech-Developer/Kratos_Agent](https://github.com/Thirumalai-Tech-Developer/Kratos_Agent/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus) · ★1)
- **gitnexus**: Knowledge of this repo's code graph - symbols, callers, callees, execution flows and the blast radius of a change. Use when the user asks "how does X work", "what breaks if I change X", "why is X failing", wants a safe rename or calls /gitnexus. This file is a switchboard: it routes to one of six sub-skills (exploring, impact analysis, debugging, refactoring, tool guide, CLI) and says what to do when the index is stale or the MCP server is missing. GitNexus is a help, never a gate - missing tools end in one line of advice, not in stopped work. ([androszr/mission-deck](https://github.com/androszr/mission-deck/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus))
- **gitnexus**: Use when navigating large codebases, analyzing blast radius before refactoring, or tracing execution flows. Optional — requires 'npx gitnexus analyze' to initialize. ([anicdh/agstack](https://github.com/anicdh/agstack/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus) · 2 copies)
- **gitnexus**: GitNexus indexes a codebase into a local knowledge graph of symbols, calls, imports, clusters and execution flows, and serves it to AI coding agents through an MCP server and a CLI. Use when a user asks to index a repo with GitNexus, set up the GitNexus MCP server for Claude Code, Cursor or Codex, check what breaks before changing a function (impact, blast radius), see how a feature flows through the code, review the impact of uncommitted changes, run Cypher queries on a code graph, or explore a repository in the GitNexus web UI. ([Atlas-Agents-Hub/muse-hub](https://github.com/Atlas-Agents-Hub/muse-hub/tree/HEAD/collections/library/skills/gitnexus) · 2 copies)
- **gitnexus**: Consulta el Grafo de Conocimiento (Knowledge Graph) AST del código fuente generado por GitNexus. Permite evaluar el radio de impacto (Blast Radius) antes de modificar símbolos, detectar llamadas upstream/downstream y mapear diffs de Git a flujos de ejecución. Usar antes de refactorizaciones o commits. ([desarrollogsi25/GLPI-TICKET-MANAGER](https://github.com/desarrollogsi25/GLPI-TICKET-MANAGER/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/gitnexus) · 2 copies)
- **gitnexus**: Code intelligence powered by GitNexus knowledge graph. Use for understanding code architecture, exploring execution flows, analyzing blast radius before changes, debugging with call graphs, safe refactoring with rename support, and CLI operations like indexing and wiki generation. ([dschmidtadv/qdrant-memory-mcp](https://github.com/dschmidtadv/qdrant-memory-mcp/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/gitnexus))
- **gitnexus**: Self-bootstrapping codebase knowledge graph. One command sets up everything: installs GitNexus, configures MCP for all editors, indexes the current repo, installs built-in skills + hooks, and verifies end-to-end. Use when: "map this repo", "repo graph", "index codebase", "gitnexus", "set up gitnexus", "what depends on X", "blast radius". Auto-activates on unfamiliar codebases. ([itsAR-VR/public-skills](https://github.com/itsAR-VR/public-skills/tree/HEAD/overrides/gitnexus) · 2 copies)
- **gitnexus**: Graph-powered code intelligence via GitNexus MCP server for impact analysis and dependency navigation. ([lfr-ai/copier-fullstack-template](https://github.com/lfr-ai/copier-fullstack-template/tree/HEAD/template/.github/skills/gitnexus))
- **gitnexus**: Use for graph-powered code exploration, impact analysis, and safer incremental refactoring. ([lfr-ai/copier-fullstack-template](https://github.com/lfr-ai/copier-fullstack-template/tree/HEAD/template/.claude/skills/gitnexus))
- **gitnexus**: Retired on 2026-11-12. GitNexus is no longer used, so do not install, index or call it; code intelligence goes through evo-kg (the kg_* tools). ([maycuatroi1/agent-skills](https://github.com/maycuatroi1/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus))
- **gitnexus**: Code intelligence via knowledge graph - impact analysis, bug tracing, architecture exploration, safe refactoring ([mozilor-technologies/mozilor-skills](https://github.com/mozilor-technologies/mozilor-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/backend/workflows/gitnexus) · 2 copies)
- **gitnexus**: Proporciona utilidades de mapeo, dependencias y análisis de arquitectura del repositorio de código local mediante GitNexus. ([PezcaderiaSAS/maestro_ERP_Pezcaderia](https://github.com/PezcaderiaSAS/maestro_ERP_Pezcaderia/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ai_agents/gitnexus))
- **gitnexus**: Code intelligence toolkit — impact analysis, code query, safe rename, execution flow tracing, and knowledge graph exploration. Use before editing any symbol, before committing, or when exploring unfamiliar code. ([RizkiRachman/workflow-state-engine](https://github.com/RizkiRachman/workflow-state-engine/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus))
- **gitnexus**: 当任务需要借助 GitNexus 代码知识图谱进行代码库探索、调用链追踪、变更影响分析、安全重构、PR 风险审查， 或需要运行 GitNexus 索引/状态/清理/wiki CLI 时使用。不要用于普通文本搜索（非符号重命名）、单文件阅读或与 GitNexus 无关的通用编程问题。 ([TomorrowLM/lm-skill](https://github.com/TomorrowLM/lm-skill/tree/HEAD/gitnexus))

- **gitnexus-assistant**: GitNexus 智能助手 - 代码库知识图谱查询工具。触发词：gitnexus、代码分析、影响分析、依赖查询、代码图谱、分析变更、查询符号、查找调用。自动识别查询意图并执行对应的 GitNexus MCP 工具。 ([Throokie/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/Throokie/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/th-gitnexus-assistant) · ★1)

- **gitnexus-bug-hunt**: Systematic architecture bug hunt using GitNexus execution flow analysis. Use whenever the user asks to find bugs, audit the codebase, discover what could crash or freeze the app, hunt security vulnerabilities, identify concurrency issues or silent failures, or says things like 'what could go wrong', 'find hidden bugs', 'security audit', 'what's risky in production', 'architecture review', or 'use GitNexus t… ([gitrdunhq/datum](https://github.com/gitrdunhq/datum/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-bug-hunt))
